2e:["$","section",null,{"className":"drh-step","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"drh-prose","children":[["$","$L2b",null,{"as":"h2","id":"while-and-for","children":"while and for"}],["$","p",null,{"children":["In a C-style ",["$","code",null,{"children":"for"}],", the first part runs once, the condition is tested before each\nround, and the last part runs after each round. Each part may be left out, and\nthe first part may declare a variable that exists only inside the loop.\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"for (T x of a..b)"}]," goes over the inclusive range from ",["$","code",null,{"children":"a"}]," to ",["$","code",null,{"children":"b"}],", and runs zero\ntimes if ",["$","code",null,{"children":"a"}]," is above ",["$","code",null,{"children":"b"}],". The step is a value of the loop's type, and it stops at the last\nvalue not past the end."]}],["$","p",null,{"children":["A negative step counts down: ",["$","code",null,{"children":"for (sint32 i of 10..0 by -2)"}]," runs for 10, 8, 6,\n4, 2 and 0. A step that points away from the end gives an empty loop, so\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"0..10 by -2"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"10..0 by 2"}]," run zero times. A literal negative step also\ncounts down over an unsigned loop variable and emits W0102. A step written as a literal\nzero is E0115. A step that is zero only when the program runs stops it at that\npoint with the run-time error ",["$","code",null,{"children":"range step is zero"}]," (exit code 101)."]}],["$","p",null,{"children":["A float range needs its ",["$","code",null,{"children":"by"}],". Value ",["$","code",null,{"children":"k"}]," is ",["$","code",null,{"children":"start + k * step"}],", computed from a\nwhole-number counter, so the values do not drift. The loop stops at the last\nvalue not past the end: ",["$","code",null,{"children":"0.0..1.0 by 0.25"}]," runs for 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75 and 1.0,\nand ",["$","code",null,{"children":"0.0..1.0 by 0.3"}]," runs for 0.0, 0.3, 0.6 and then the third step, which is\n0.9 up to float rounding.
